# Ladybird commit inventory: September 10–19, 2026

This is the complete 907-commit decision ledger for the
[translation audit](LADYBIRD_2026-09-10_2026-09-19.md). Dates and order use UTC
committer timestamps. Each immutable upstream link exposes the full message and
changed paths. The selected-source matrix and integration/validation limits are
in the main audit.

Final statuses reconcile overlapping subsystem reviews; selected port mappings
take precedence, and no unresolved delegated entries remain. “Already covered”
describes the reviewed behavior, not a claim of complete subsystem parity.

Review depth:

- **Exact diff:** upstream production changes were compared with the matching
  Vox implementation. This is source review, not a runtime validation result.
- **Owner review:** commit message/paths and the matching Vox source or
  architecture were reviewed; the complete upstream diff was not exhaustively
  compared.
- **Message/paths:** commit title/message and changed-path screening. Test-only
  and tooling exclusions may be decided at this level.

| Disposition | Commits |
| --- | ---: |
| Ported | 67 |
| Partially ported | 3 |
| Already covered | 50 |
| Deferred | 492 |
| Not applicable | 230 |
| Tests/tooling skipped | 65 |
| **Total** | **907** |
